Typical sizes ≥ 1. Use:\n```\nconst size_t row = nx;\nconst size_t plane = (size_t)nx*ny;\nconst size_t stepx_p = (x + 1 < nx) ? 1 : 0;\nconst size_t stepx_n = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\nconst size_t stepy_p = (y + 1 < ny) ? row : 0;\n...\n```\nThen c[idx + stepx_p] etc. That's efficient.\n\nThe original clamped condition uses `x < nx-1`, which for nx=0 underflow size_t. nx>=1 in practice. But using same pointer for read and write with __restrict__ is UB. So either no restrict on the in-place pointer, or maintain two buffers and swap. Two buffers but update into other buffer then swap: that uses 2 arrays total: c and mu? Wait if we allocate mu and cnew separately, then cold is third unless we reuse. Let's re-derive with 2 arrays and no in-place:\n\nOption A: arrays A and B.\n- compute mu into B from A.\n- update A in place using A center and B mu.\nRequires in-place and non-restrict.\n\nOption B: arrays A and B as concentration ping-pong, plus mu? Need 3 arrays. Unless mu overlaps with one concentration buffer cleverly:\n- Compute mu into cnew buffer B from cold A.\n- Update output into ... A? in-place again.\nAlternatively:\n- Compute mu into B from A.\n- Update writes into A in place. Same.\n\nOption C: Can update write into B while reading mu from B? No, would overwrite mu neighbors before read.\n\nSo to use 2 arrays we need in-place update. Then pass `double* c` with `__restrict__` and read/write same pointer; remove restrict from that pointer. That's fine. But does in-place break due to threads reading neighbor cold? No, update only reads cold[idx] (own cell) and mu at neighbors. Wait update kernel calls computeLaplacian(mu), so only mu neighbors. cold only at idx. So safe.\n\nBut is there any benefit to using two concentration buffers for correctness of the \"swap\" semantics? In-place yields same values.\n\nAlternatively, we can allocate 3 arrays if memory permits and avoid in-place, but 2 is safer memory-wise and same speed (same traffic). \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* file, int line) {\n    if (err != cudaSuccess) {\n        std::fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", file, line, cudaGetErrorString(err));\n        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);\n    }\n}\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(expr) cudaCheck((expr), __FILE__, __LINE__)\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\n__device__ __forceinline__ double computeLaplacian(const double* __restrict__ c,\n                                                   const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                                   const double invDx2, const double invDy2, const double invDz2,\n                                                   const int x, const int y, const int z) {\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;\n\n    const size_t xp = (x + 1 < nx) ? 1 : 0;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? static_cast<size_t>(nx) : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? static_cast<size_t>(nx) : 0;\n    const double cyy = (c[idx + yp] + c[idx - yn] - 2.0 * cv) * invDy2;\n    const double czz = (c[idx + zp] + c[idx - zn] - 2.0 * cv) * invDz2;\n\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\n__global__ void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                               const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                               const double invDx2, const double invDy2, const double invDz2,\n                                               const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = blockIdx.z * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t idx = mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n            - gamma * lap;\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\n__global__ void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(double* c, const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                         const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                         const double D, const double dt,\n                                         const double invDx2, const double invDy2, const double invDz2) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = blockIdx.z * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t ny, nz, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2, x, y, z);\n\n    c[idx] = c[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c,\n return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;\n    const unsigned long long linear_id = static_cast<unsigned long long>(idx);\n    const double pseudo = static_cast<double>(((linear_id + 1ull) * 1299709ull) % vol) /\n                          static_cast<double>(vol);\n    c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nstatic inline dim3 makeBlockDim() {\n    return dim3(32, 8, 4);\n}\n\nstatic inline dim3 makeGridDim(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const dim3 block = makeBlockDim();\n    return dim3(static_cast<unsigned int>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x),\n                static_cast<unsigned int>((ny + block.y - 1) / * nx + x;\n    const size_t offxp = (x + 1 < nx) ? 1 : 0;\n    const size_t offxn = (x > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    const size_t offyp = (y + 1 < ny) ? static_cast<size_t>(nx) : 0;\n    const size_t offyn = (y > 0) ? static_cast<size_t>(nx) : 0;\n\n    #pragma unroll\n    for (int k = 0; k < ZCOARSEN; ++k) {\n        const int z = z0 + k;\n        if (z >= nz) {\n            break;\n        }\n        const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + base;\n        const size_t offzp = (z + 1 < nz) ? plane : 0;\n        const size_t offzn = (z > 0) ? plane : 0;\n\n        const double cv = mu[idx];\n        const double s = mu[idx + offxp] + mu[idx - offxn]\n                       + mu[idx + offyp] + mu[idx - offyn]\n                       + mu[idx + offzp] + mu[idx - offzn];\n\n        c[idx] = c[idx] + dtD * (s - 6.0 * cv);\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c,\n                                              const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                              const unsigned long long vol) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = blockIdx.z * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;\n    const unsigned long long linear_id = static_cast<unsigned long long>(idx);\n    const double pseudo = static_cast<double>(((linear_id + 1ull) * 1299709ull) % vol) /\n                          static_cast<double>(vol);\n    c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nstatic inline dim3 makeBlockDim() {\n    return dim3(32, 8, 1);\n}\n\nstatic inline dim3 makeGridDim(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const dim3 block = makeBlockDim();\n    return dim3(static_cast<unsigned int>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x),\n                static_cast<unsigned int>((ny + block.y - 1) / block.y),\n                static_cast<unsigned int>((nz + static_cast<size_t>(block.z) * ZCOARSEN - 1) /\n                                          (static_cast<size_t>(block.z) * ZCOARSEN)));\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n    \n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = source_root, benchmark: benchmark,\n                                                stage_root: File.join(output_dir, \"source\"))\n      detected = begin\n        parallelization_detection(File.dirname(staged_source), benchmark)\n      rescue Errno::ENOENT => e\n        result.err_string = \"Error during parallelization detection: #{e.message}\"\n        messages << result.err_string\n        return finish_result(result_path, messages, result)\n      end\n      semantic_ok = par_type == PAR_HYBRID ? !detected.empty? : detected == [par_type]\n      unless semantic_ok\n        result.err_string = if detected.empty?\n          \"Error during parallelization detection: No parallelization approach detected in source code.\"\n        else\n          \"Error during parallelization detection: Detected parallelization approaches #{detected} do not match target #{par_type}.\"\n        end\n        messages << result.err_string\n        return finish_result(result_path, messages, result)\n      end\n      result.basic_para = true\n      messages << \"Parallelization detection PASSED: Detected parallelization approaches #{detected}.\"\n\n      result.err_string = \"Error during validation build:\\n#{build_error}. See validation_out_*.log.\"\n        messages << result.err_string\n        return finish_result(result_path, messages, result)\n      end\n      result.validation_run = true\n      messages << \"Validation run PASSED.\"\n\n      unless output.include?(\"Validation: PASSED\") && !internal_failure?(output)\n        result.err_string = \"Internal validation FAILED.\"\n        messages << result.err_string\n        return finish_result(result_path, messages, result)\n      end\n      result.internal_validation = true\n      messages << \"Internal validation PASSED.\"\n\n      comparison = validate(reference, output, benchmark)\n      unless comparison[0]\n        result.err_string = \"Output comparison FAILED:\\n#{comparison[1]}\"\n        messages << result.err_string\n        return finish_result(result_path, messages, result)\n      end\n      result.output_comparison = true\n      messages << \"Output comparison PASSED:\\n#{comparison[1]}\"\n      finish_result(result_path, messages, result)\n    rescue InfrastructureError, SystemCallError, IOError => e\n      # Infrastructure failures intentionally remain absent from the aggregate result list.\n      LocalEvaluation.atomic_write(File.join(output_dir, \"infrastructure_error.log\"), \"#{e.class}: #{e.message}\\n\") if output_dir\n      raise\n    rescue StandardError => e\n      # Detection/build/output errors caused by generated source are reproducible program failures.\n      result ||= ValidationResult.new(benchmark, model, par_type, run_number)\n      result.err_string = \"Validation pipeline error: #{e.class}: #{e.message}\"\n      messages ||= []\n      messages << result.err_string\n      finish_result(result_path, messages, result)\n    end\n\n    def finish_result(path, messages, result)\n      LocalEvaluation.atomic_write(path, messages.join(\"\\n\") + \"\\n\")\n      LocalEvaluation.atomic_yaml(File.join(File.dirname(path), \"validation_metadata.yaml\"), {\n        \"id\" => result.id_string,\n        \"benchmark\" => result.benchmark,\n        \"model\" => result.model,\n        \"par_type\" => result.par_type,\n        \"run\" => result.run,\n        \"manifest_sha256\" => LocalEvaluation.sha256_file(@manifest.path),\n        \"stages\" => {\n","exit":0,"truncated":false},"title":"sed -n '1,260p' /tmp/luna6-opencode-medium-generation-20261019-3445387-64htcj/experiment/lib/local_evaluation/validation_pipeline.rb","time":{"start":1792679249299,"end":1792679249336}},"id":"prt_164028562001Hej5ZqSs32i5qD","sessionID":"ses_e9c110df7ffexA2Ea6euQGCbI0","messageID":"msg_164027325001eHbuuZjBdJx2K4"}}
